Second transcription's note: The kidnapped question is needed only when the regular tests do not already make the person your qualifying child. A known yes always refuses, and an unanswered question does not block a child who already qualifies.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 21, Multiple support agreements: "If no one person contributed over half of the support of your relative (or a person who lived with you all year as a member of your household) but you and another person(s) provided more than half of your relative’s support, special rules may apply"; i1040gi p. 17, Step 1 (a qualifying child is tested on relationship, age, own support, joint return and residency; no support test of yours). The reply still declares dependent.multiple_support on dependent.claimed itself, so it refuses a qualifying child whose support was shared. Compared with its round-3 reply, the reply restores the Form 2441 death-year route (stage15.f2441.parent_died_part_year now agrees: dc_qp yes, dc_count 1, line 2(d) $4,000.00, line 11 $600.00 on both sides) and adds the born, died and placed route to its qualifying-child residency test (dependent.h_residency). executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,487 partial records; 7,463 comparisons): 6,650 of 7,463 agree; 317 project-refuses, 279 candidate-unknown, 117 candidate-refuses, 99 project-unknown, 1 value; on returns the full interview completes: 83 candidate-refuses, 29 candidate-unknown. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-household/dependent.full.json, causes assigned by /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-household/classify.py, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 83 candidate-refuses: dependent.multiple_support refused on a qualifying child, all classm.s3.4 dep_a (a child aged 2, 12 months, multiple_support yes; the fixtures classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek and 81 partial records of classm.s3.4); the project claims the child (multiple support is refused only on dependent.qualifying_relative); (b) 3 candidate-unknown dependent.lawful on fixtures whose household_member entries predate that question (classm.clcw26.2 and .deepseek dep_b, stage8.single.dependent.household_and_ssn all_year); (c) 6 candidate-unknown dependent.born_died: the reply's dependent.h_residency asks it of every person with months under 7, but dependent.months already folds in the birth, death and placement exception ("For a person born, died, adopted or placed with you in 2025 who meets the other conditions to be your qualifying child, enter 12 if your home was their home for more than half the time they were alive or with you"; i1040gi p. 21, Exception to time lived with you), so the interview never asks it there (classm.f8960.2 and .deepseek dep_a, audit.dependent.listed_not_dependent_no_tin child, generated 64, 100, 405); an answer the source does not need; (d) 20 partial records withholding an answer the reply needs first (19 multiple_support, 1 divorced_rule). Off completed returns, the 317 project-refuses are its cited dependent screens (claimed_no_tin and those of dependent.qualifying_child) on returns refused anyway. BUG-0258 rewords dependent.parent_home_paid only; this line does not read it. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: This is the per-person condition only. The cost of keeping up the home is a household fact (household.home_cost) and is not encoded here. Items 1(a), 1(c), 3 and 4 of Test 2 fall under the refusals on dependent.claimed.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 14, Test 2 (a qualifying person who lived with you more than half the year; your qualifying child or a qualifying relative related as listed; item 2, your unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent); p501 p. 9, Death or birth; i1040gi p. 21, Multiple support agreements: "If no one person contributed over half of the support of your relative (or a person who lived with you all year as a member of your household) but you and another person(s) provided more than half of your relative’s support, special rules may apply"; i1040gi p. 17, Step 1 (a qualifying child is tested on relationship, age, own support, joint return and residency; no support test of yours). The per-person test (residency, then a dependent qualifying child or listed relative, or an unmarried qualifying child who is not your dependent) is the project's, and its note leaves the cost of the home to household.home_cost, as the project does (BUG-0258 rewords that question only). It inherits its dependent.claimed. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,487 partial records; 7,463 comparisons): 6,861 of 7,463 agree; 317 project-refuses, 117 candidate-refuses, 100 project-unknown, 68 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 83 candidate-refuses, 23 candidate-unknown, 3 project-unknown. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-household/dependent.full.json, causes assigned by /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-household/classify.py, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 83 candidate-refuses: dependent.multiple_support refused on a qualifying child, all classm.s3.4 dep_a (a child aged 2, 12 months, multiple_support yes; the fixtures classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek and 81 partial records of classm.s3.4); the project claims the child (multiple support is refused only on dependent.qualifying_relative); (b) 3 candidate-unknown dependent.born_died: the reply's dependent.h_residency asks it of every person with months under 7, but dependent.months already folds in the birth, death and placement exception ("For a person born, died, adopted or placed with you in 2025 who meets the other conditions to be your qualifying child, enter 12 if your home was their home for more than half the time they were alive or with you"; i1040gi p. 21, Exception to time lived with you), so the interview never asks it there (classm.f8960.2 and .deepseek dep_a, audit.dependent.listed_not_dependent_no_tin child); an answer the source does not need; (c) 20 partial records withholding an answer the reply needs (19 multiple_support, 1 citizen); (d) 3 project-unknown partial records of classm.scveh.1 dep_a withholding divorced_rule, married or other_claimant. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 2, Who Can Claim an Education Credit: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student"; i8863 p. 3, item 5: "Filers and students must have been issued a TIN by the due date of their 2025 return (including extensions)" (amb.2025.edu_filer_tin); i8863 p. 9, Line 31. Its lifetime learning test (dependent.h_edu_llc) has no filer-TIN or f8863.paid test; in the constructed jack_kay witness above it gives line 31 $0.00 where the project gives $4,300.00. executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/dependent.json`; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,487 partial records; 7,463 comparisons): 3,326 of 7,463 agree; 3,811 candidate-unknown, 322 candidate-refuses, 3 project-refuses, 1 project-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 1,100 candidate-unknown, 83 candidate-refuses. Every completed-return difference was enumerated with the witness cap lifted (triage.mjs run directly; scratch /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-household/dependent.full.json, causes assigned by /tmp/adj-fixr2-dependent-household/classify.py, none unassigned; the uncapped rerun reproduces the recorded counts exactly): (a) 83 candidate-refuses: dependent.multiple_support refused on a qualifying child, all classm.s3.4 dep_a (a child aged 2, 12 months, multiple_support yes; the fixtures classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek and 81 partial records of classm.s3.4); the project claims the child (multiple support is refused only on dependent.qualifying_relative); (b) 1,100 candidate-unknown dependent.edu on returns with f8863.paid no, where the project hides the line and does not ask it (for example classm.f2441.1.deepseek a). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One, for counting listed persons"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}
  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
  - id: dependent.h_age_test
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 19 and younger than you; or under age 24, a full-time student and younger than you; or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
                - op: test
                  all:
                    - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_residency
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025, or for more than half the part of the year the person was alive or since the adoption or placement"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.born_died, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.placed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.part_year_home, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1: meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_residency, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_rel_listed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: related to you in a way that does not require living with you all year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr_member
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: a listed relative, or any other person who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_listed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.months, {const: 12, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 1: meets the conditions to be your qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    note: "The sheltered workshop exception to the gross income test has no input. The test uses the gross income entered, without that exclusion."
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr_member, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.other_claimant, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_citizen_test
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A U.S. citizen, U.S. national, U.S. resident alien, or a resident of Canada or Mexico"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_filer_can_claim
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are filing a joint return, or you could not be claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return"
    type: Bool
    note: "sa.dependent also covers a spouse and dual-status aliens, which is wider than Step 2, question 4. It is the only available input for that question, and a yes answer is treated as the STOP in that question."
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_kidnap_open
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A presumed-kidnapped child whom the regular tests do not already make your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 18, type: Count}]}
  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent (Steps 1, 2 and 4)"
    type: Bool
    note: "The kidnapped question is needed only when the regular tests do not already make the person your qualifying child. A known yes always refuses, and an unanswered question does not block a child who already qualifies."
    refuses:
      - dependent.divorced_rule
      - dependent.married
      - dependent.multiple_support
      - dependent.other_claimant
      - dependent.h_kidnap_open
      - {when: dependent.kidnapped, when_known: true}
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_citizen_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_filer_can_claim, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: you can claim the child tax credit for this person"
    type: Bool
    note: "household.ctc_ssn is read as the filer condition in Step 3, question 4 and in Before you begin. Its computation is not supplied."
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 and 5: you can claim the credit for other dependents for this person"
    type: Bool
    note: "household.all_tin is read as the condition that you, and your spouse if filing jointly, have an SSN or ITIN. Whether it counts a pending ITIN application is not shown in the catalog."
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_ctc_each
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,200 for each qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's part of Schedule 8812, line 5"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_ctc_each}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
  - id: dependent.h_odc_each
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$500 for each other dependent"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}
  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's part of Schedule 8812, line 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_odc_each}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
  - id: dependent.h_actc_each
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,700 for each qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}
  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person's part of Schedule 8812, line 16b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_actc_each}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 2: a person who lived in the home for more than half of the year and is a qualifying dependent or an unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent"
    type: Bool
    note: "This is the per-person condition only. The cost of keeping up the home is a household fact (household.home_cost) and is not encoded here. Items 1(a), 1(c), 3 and 4 of Test 2 fall under the refusals on dependent.claimed."
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_residency, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                    - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_listed, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.married, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 1: you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home that was the main home for all of 2025 of your parent whom you can claim as a dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_kidnap_open_eic
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A presumed-kidnapped child who does not otherwise meet the EIC residency test"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.kidnapped, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {const: 18, type: Count}]}
  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Step 3: meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    refuses:
      - dependent.divorced_rule
      - dependent.married
      - dependent.other_claimant
      - dependent.h_kidnap_open_eic
      - {when: dependent.kidnapped, when_known: true}
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_test, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A qualifying child for the EIC who has a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_under13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Under age 13 when the care was provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 2441 qualifying person: your dependent qualifying child under age 13, or your dependent who wasn't able to care for themselves and lived with you for more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    note: "This does not depend on the amount paid, because the instructions treat a person with no expenses as a qualifying person for the $6,000 limit. The case of a disabled person who is not your dependent is left to f2441.nondependent_gate."
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_residency, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_dc_under13, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_one_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent, used to turn the qualifying person test into a count"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}
  - id: dependent.h_dc_flag
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent if a qualifying person, otherwise zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_one_cent}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Form 2441 qualifying persons this entry counts for (1 or 0)"
    type: Count
    note: "choose cannot return a Count, so 1 or 0 is produced by percent_floor as 100 x (1 cent or 0) / 100 cents."
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: dependent.h_dc_flag, whole: {param: sc.hundred_cents}, cap: 1}
  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This dependent's modified AGI counted on Worksheet 1-2 (Form 8962, line 2b), only if the dependent is required to file an income tax return because their income meets the filing threshold"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero
  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.dc_paid}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    note: "Age at the end of 2025 settles the point when it is below 13 or above 13. At exactly 13 the answer comes from dependent.dc_over12_care and stays unknown if that is unanswered. The instructions are open on a person over 12 with no expenses, and the box is checked for such a person because the person is over 12 and disabled."
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, gt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_over_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An academic period's assistance and refunds were more than its expenses, so the Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet cannot be done as one total"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26: complete lines 27 through 30 for this student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Go to line 31 for this student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet, line 3: total adjustments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc_expenses
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet line 1 for the American opportunity credit: tuition, required fees and course materials"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_inst, dependent.edu_mat]}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_ws4_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet line 4 for the American opportunity credit: subtract line 3 from line 1; if zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.h_edu_aoc_expenses, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_ws4_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet line 4 for the lifetime learning credit: subtract line 3 from line 1; if zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_inst, take: dependent.h_edu_adj}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_line27_figured
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [dependent.h_edu_ws4_aoc, dependent.h_edu_cap]}
  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_edu_line27_figured}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: dependent.edu_line27, take: {param: aoc.full_band}}
  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: dependent.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}
  - id: dependent.h_edu_line29_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_line29, dependent.h_edu_band]}
  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.edu_line27}
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [dependent.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}, then: dependent.h_edu_line29_plus}
  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_refused]
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}, then: dependent.h_edu_ws4_llc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}, then: dependent.h_zero}
```
